[\[37a.5\]]na srog lta bu'o/_/de la bsam gtan zhes pa ni rnal 'byor te sems pa'o/_/bsam gtan du bya ba zhes pa ni bsam pa de'i 'bras bu gang yin pa ste de ni bsam gtan gyi sgo nas 'jug pa'o/_/dngos grub sgrub pa ni rab dang 'bring dang tha ma la sogs pa ste de dag sgrub pa'i phyir 
[\[37a.6\]]'jug pa de ni sngags sgrub pa'i sgor zhugs pa'o/_/yi ge'i 'bru rnams kyang kun rdzob dang don dam pa'i sgo nas srog la sogs par 'gyur ba ste/_de la yi ge thams cad kyi 'tsho bar gyur pa zhes pa ni byang chub kyi sgo nas yi ge rnams a la brten pa'i phyir 'tsho bar gyur pa ste/_
[\[37a.7\]]a zhes pa'i don ni ma skyes pa yin la/_de bzhin gshegs pa zhes bya ba'i don kyang dgos pa thams cad dang bral ba ste ma skyes pa'i don to/_/de bas na yi ge ka byang chub kyi sa bon gyi 'tsho ba yang a la rag lus pa'i phyir srog ces bya'o/_/de bzhin du yi ge lhag ma rnams kyang shes par 
[\[37b\]]
[\[37b.1\]]bya'o/_/de la yi ge thams cad kyi srog tu gyur pa zhes pa ni spyod pa'i sgo nas yig 'bru rnams de la brten pas na de'i phyir srog ces bya'o/_/skyabs su gyur pa zhes pa ni kun du sangs rgyas pa'i sgo nas yi ge rnams kyi rten yin pas na skyabs so/_/byung ngo zhes pa ni mya ngan 'das pa'i 
[\[37b.2\]]sgo nas yi ge rnams byung ba'i don to/_/rigs kyi bu dag 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis byin gyis brlabs pa zhes pa ni sangs rgyas kyi sku'i 'khor lo thams cad kyis byin gyis brlabs zhes pa'i don to/_/byang chub sems dpa' gsang sngags kyi sgo nas byang 
[\[37b.3\]]chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa rnams la/_sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pas so sor nye bar gnas pa yin te zhes pa ni yi ge a de'i mthu chen po bstan pa ste sangs rgyas 'jig rten du 'byung ba'i tshe chos rnam pa gnyis bstan pa ni sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa ste/_rnam pa gnyis ni bsam 
[\[37b.4\]]pa dag pa rnams la rtogs pa'i chos bstan pa dang bsam pa ma dag pa rnams la lung gi chos bstan pa'o/_/de bzhin du yi ge a yang chos de gnyi ga ston pa'i rgyu yin te/_de bas na sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pas so sor nye bar gnas pa yin no/_/ji ltar zhe na/_rtogs pa'i chos 
[\[37b.5\]]rnams kyang yi ge a de'i don ma skyes pa las byung la/_lung gi chos rnams kyang yi ge'i 'bru'i sgo nas bshad pa yin te/_tshig thams cad yi ge a las ma gyur pa med pa'i phyir ro/_/sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa byed par ji ltar shes she na/_chos thams cad ni a zhes bya ba'i 
[\[37b.6\]]sgo nas 'jug go zhes gsungs te/_de yang ji ltar zhe na/_yi ge a zhes bya bas rgyu byas pa las rtogs pa'i chos dang lung gi chos thams cad 'byung ba'i phyir ro/_/de ltar yi ge a zhes bya ba de sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa byed cing chos thams cad 'byung ba'i sgo yin pa de lta bas na sngags la 
[\[37b.7\]]spyod pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi yi ge a de de bzhin gshegs par blta ba la sogs pa bya ste/_de la de bzhin gshegs pa rnams mthong bar 'dod pas de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi snying po 'di la brtson par bya'o zhes sbyar te/_yi ge a zhes bya ba'i don ni de bzhin 
[\[38a\]]
[\[38a.1\]]gshegs pa'i rang bzhin las gzhan ma yin pa'i phyir snying po byang chub nyid kyi snying po ste de ni don dam pa'o/_/kun rdzob tu yang a zhes bya ba'i yi ge'i 'bru de bsgoms na de'i mthus de bzhin gshegs pa grangs med pa 'char ba'i phyir ro/_/de bzhin gshegs 
[\[38a.2\]]pa mchod par 'dod pas kyang yi ge 'di bsgom par bya la mchod pa yang sgrub pa'i mchod pa dang /_zang zing gi mchod pa rnam pa gnyis su rig par bya'o/_/de la sgrub pa'i mchod pa ni yi ge a de nyid kyi don bsgoms par 'gyur la/_zang zing gi mchod pa ni yi ge a de bsgoms pa'i mthu las 
[\[38a.3\]]nam mkha' gang ba'i mchod pa rgya chen po 'byung ba'i phyir ro/_/byang chub kyi sems mngon sum du bya bar 'dod pa zhes pa ni byang chub sems dpa'i sa dang po yan chad kyi sems la bya ste/_de mngon du bya bar 'dod pas yi ge a'i don mngon par sgrub par byos shig ces pa'i don to/_/de ci'i 
[\[38a.4\]]phyir zhe na/_byang chub kyi sems kyang ma skyes pa yin pas yi ge a'i don ma skyes pa dang mtshungs pa'i phyir ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams dang 'grogs par 'dod pa zhes pa ni de ltar 'dod pas yi ge a'i sgo sgoms shig ces 'don te/_byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyang ma skyes pa'i 
[\[38a.5\]]don la dmigs/_yi ge a yang ma skyes pa'i don yin pas na spyod yul mthun pas lhan cig tu skye bar 'gyur ro/_/de yan chad ni bsam gtan gyi sgo nas bsgom pa dang /_a nyid sngags kyi sgo sgrub pa la zhugs pa gnyis ka'i thun mong gi yon tan bstan pa yin la dngos grub mngon 
[\[38a.6\]]par 'dod pa zhes pa ni sngags kyi sgo nas zhugs pa'i grub pa rab 'bring rnam pa gsum mo/_/de rnams ni yan lag gi tshig yin la/_rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid thob par 'dod pas 'di sgoms shig ces bya ba ni bsdus pa'i don yin no/_/de nas dkyil 'khor bri ba'i cho ga rnam 
[\[38a.7\]]pa gsum bstan pa'i phyir/_de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par mdzad kyis snying rje chen po'i snying po 'byung ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi rgyal po 'di'i cho ga dgod pa dang /_lha'i gnas gtan la dbab pa dang zhes bya ba la sogs pa gsungs te/_de la snying rje chen po'i snying po 'byung ba zhes pa 
[\[38b\]]
[\[38b.1\]]ni lus kyi 'khor los byin gyis brlabs pa'i dkyil 'khor 'di dang /_ngag gi dkyil 'khor la yang ming du gdags so/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_dkyil 'khor rnam pa gsum ka yang snying rje chen po'i snying po'i nus pa dang rgyus dkyil 'khor 'byung ba'i phyir de skad ces bya'o/_/lha'i 
[\[38b.2\]]gnas gtan la dbab pa zhes pa ni dbung mtha' dang mchog [(]du [,]tu [)]bkod pa'i lha bzhugs pa'i gnas nges par bya'o/_/ting nge 'dzin rnam par sprul pa zhes bya ba ni gang ting nge 'dzin du zhugs nas srung ba'i rig pa dang sngags la sogs pa'o/_/gsang sngags spyod pa zhes pa ni dkyil 'khor 
[\[38b.3\]]du 'jug pa'i tshe las su spyad pa rnams so/_/bsam par bya ba ni dkyil 'khor du 'jug pa na slob mas byang chub kyi sems bsam pa dang /_slob dpon rang gi ting nge 'dzin du gnas pa la sogs pa'i bye brag gsungs pa'o/_/de dag so so'i bshad pa ni phyis 'chad par 'gyur ro/_/
[\[38b.4\]]de la dkyil 'khor gyi rgyal po 'di'i cho ga dgod pa zhes don bshad pa'i phyir/_de la slob dpon gyis thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi sgo a zhes bya ba la gnas te zhes pa nas/_de bzhin gshegs pa'i gzugs snying po nyid kyi rang gi ngo bor byin gyis brlabs la zhes pa'i bar du gsungs te/_
[\[38b.5\]]de la dkyil 'khor gyi thig bre ba'i tshe dkyil 'khor gyi snam bu re re la yang slob dpon gyi ting nge 'dzin re re gsungs te/_phyi rol gyi rim pa'i dkyil 'khor la byang chub sems dpa' 'jam dpal la sogs pa pha rol tu phyin pa las skyes pa rnams bzhugs pas na thig bri ba'i slob 
[\[38b.6\]]dpon gyi ting nge 'dzin kyang thams cad mkhyen pa'i rgyu'i gnas kyi snying po yi ge a la gnas te dkyil 'khor gyi srad bu bres shig ces gsungs pa yin no/_/de la dang po thig bri ba'i tshe dang por nam mkha'i thig bri bar gsungs pa ni sangs rgyas thams cad la phyag 'tshal nas zhes 
[\[38b.7\]]pa nas byang phyogs su kha bltas nas srad bu bres te zhes pa'i bar gyis so/_/de nas sa'i dkyil 'khor gyi thig bri ba'i phyir/_/nub phyogs su kha bltas la bsgyur te thig gdab po zhes gsungs pa yin no/_/lhag ma rnams 'dir ma gsungs pa ni snying rje chen po'i dkyil 'khor du 
[\[39a\]]
[\[39a.1\]]thig bri ba zhib tu bshad zin pas 'dir yang lhag ma rnams de dang 'dra bar shes par bya'o/_/de nas snying po'i dkyil 'khor gyi thig bri ba'i slob dpon gyi ting nge 'dzin bshad pa'i phyir/_de nas bdag nyid rdo rje sems dpa' lag na rdo rjer de'i phyag rgya'am/_
[\[39a.2\]]yi ge ba zhes bya bas byin gyis brlabs te nang du zhugs la snying po'i dkyil 'khor bri bar bya'i zhes gsungs te/_de'i slob dpon gyi ting nge 'dzin ni byang chub pa'i sa bon yi ge as bskyed pa'i rdo rje sems dpar slob dpon bdag nyid byin gyis brlab pa'o/_/rdo rje sems dpa' la yang 
[\[39a.3\]]las kyi rdo rje sems dpa' la sogs pa bzhin du 'dren pa yang yod de/_skabs 'dir ni gzhan la mi bya'i rdo rje sems dpa' phyag na rdo rje la bya'o/_/de nas dkyil 'khor bar ma'i snam bu'i thig bri ba'i ting nge 'dzin bstan pa'i phyir/_/de bzhin du dkyil 'khor gnyis pa la yang 
[\[39a.4\]]zhes pa nas/_snying po nyid kyi rang gi ngo bor byin gyis brlabs la zhes pa'i bar du gsungs te/_de la dkyil 'khor snam bu bar ma'i nang na longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku dang sprul pa'i sku gnyis bzhugs pas dkyil 'khor de'i thig bri ba'i tshe'ang sku de gnyis kyi rgyu'i ting nge 'dzin 
[\[39a.5\]]rnam pa bzhi slob dpon byin gyis brlabs te/_slob dpon gyis ting nge 'dzin de rnams dmigs nas bya'o zhes pa'i don to/_/de nas gzod ma nas zhi ba ni byang chub pa'o/_/gnyis su med pa'i rnal 'byor gyi gzugs ni spyod pa'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i gzugs ni kun du 
[\[39a.6\]]sangs rgyas pa'o/_/snying po nyid kyi rang gi ngo bo ni mya ngan las 'das pa'o/_/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i gnas pa mngon du byed cing zhes pa nas/_lha'i gnas bsham par bya'o zhes pa'i bar gyis de'i rgyud nas bshad pa gnas gtan la phab pa yin te/_phyis kyang 'chad do/_/
[\[39a.7\]]de la snam bu gcig las kyang gsum du bgos te/_sum cha la 'khor sa bya zhing sum gnyis la lha'i gzugs bya'o zhes pa'i don to/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i gnas pa mngon du byed cing srad bu bres la zhes pas de ltar lha'i gnas dang 'khor sa dgod pa gya tshom du mi bya bar thig 
[\[39b\]]
[\[39b.1\]]btab ste bya bas de nyid kyi tshe na yang de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod pa stong pa nyid la dmigs par bya'o zhes pa'i don to/_/de nas dkyil 'khor gyi tshon rtsi rnams byin gyis brlab pa'i cho ga bstan pa'i phyir/_de nas yang bdag nyid bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad du de'i phyag rgyas byin 
[\[39b.2\]]gyis brlabs la zhes pa nas/_bdud sde sdang ba thams cad la/_/rnam pa kun du bsdigs par byed/_/ces pa'i bar du gsungs pa yin te/_de la bdag nyid bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad du de'i phyag rgyas byin gyis brlabs la zhes pa ni tshon rnams byin gyis rlob pa'i tshe rdo 
[\[39b.3\]]rje'i phyag rgyas slob dpon nyid rnam par snang mdzad kyi skur byin gyis rlobs shig ces pa ste/_rdo rje ni ye shes kyi mtshan ma yin pas na ye shes lngas byin gyis brlabs pa'i don to/_/tshon rnams byin gyis brlab pa yang kun rdzob dang don dam pa rnam pa gnyis te/_de la don dam par byin 
[\[39b.4\]]gyis brlab pa'i phyir/_chos kyi dbyings rgya chen por byin gyis brlabs te zhes gsungs pa yin te/_des ni mtshon sna lnga po rnams chos kyi dbyings rgya chen po stong pa nyid du byin gyis brlab bo zhes bstan to/_/thog mar tshon rtsi dkar po blangs la zhes pas ni kun rdzob kyi sgo nas tshon re 
[\[39b.5\]]re zhing yang lha re res byin gyis brlab par gsungs pa yin te/_de la tshon dkar po ni chos kyi dbyings kyis byin gyis brlab par gsungs pa la/_chos kyi dbyings de'i rang bzhin dang mthu bstan pa'i phyir/_'di ni chos dbyings dag pa ste/_sems can khams ni rnam sbyong ba/_/
[\[39b.6\]]'di ni bdag nyid de bzhin gshegs/_/nyes pa thams cad rnam par spangs/_/zhes gsungs pa yin no/_/chos kyi dbyings kyi don de lta bur bsams nas don de yi ger 'bar ba la sogs pa'i yon tan bshad pa de lta bus byin gyis brlab po/_/de nas tshon dmar po byin gyis brlab 
[\[39b.7\]]pa'i phyir/_de nas tshon rtsi gnyis pa ni/_/zhes bya ba la sogs pa gsungs te/_yi ge a'i sa bon gyis bskyed pa'i sangs rgyas thub dkas byin gyis brlab par gsungs so/_/de nas tshon ser po la yi ge ka'i sa bon gyis bskyed pa'i/_sangs rgyas gser thub kyis byin gyis brlab 
[\[40a\]]
[\[40a.1\]]pa'i phyir/_de nas sngags pas tshon gsum pa/_/zhes pa la sogs pa gsungs pa yin no/_/tshon ljang gu la snying po byang chub na bzhugs pa'i skus byin gyis brlab pa'i phyir/_de nas margad 'dra ba yi/_/zhes pa la sogs pa gsungs pa yin te/_tshon ljang gu'i kha 
[\[40a.2\]]dog ni nor bu margad kyi kha dog lta bu'o/_/tshon nag po khams gsum rnam par rgyal bas byin gyis brlab pa'i phyir/_de nas tshon rtsi nag po ste/_zhes bya ba la sogs pa gsungs pa yin te/_de la yi ge has bskyed pa'i khro bo'i gzugs de yang ci 'dra zhe na/_'bar ba'i phreng ba rnam par 
[\[40a.3\]]'phro/_/dus mtha'i me ltar mi bzad pa/_/zhes pa la sogs pa'o/_/de yang tshon sna lnga rnams ye shes lnga'i rang bzhin du blta ste dkar po ni chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin no/_/dmar po ni sangs rgyas thub dka'i rang bzhin te me long lta bu'o/_/ser po ni mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes so/_/
[\[40a.4\]]ljang gu ni so sor kun du rtog pa'i ye shes so/_/nag po ni bya ba sgrub pa'i ye shes so/_/de dag gi rim pa bshad pa ni dang por chos kyi dbyings nyid du 'jug la/_stong pa 'ba' zhig tu dmigs par yang mi rung ste snying rje chen po'i dbang gis sems can la dmigs shing chags pa 
[\[40a.5\]]bskyed pa'i phyir de'i 'og tu tshon dmar po dgye'o/_/sems can gyi don la zhugs pas rgyas par 'gyur te/_de bas na de'i 'og tu tshon ser po bri'o/_/rgyas shing mthu dang ldan pas na las thams cad nus pa ste/_bdag dang gzhan gyi don nus pas de'i 'og tu tshon ljang gu bri'o/_/
[\[40a.6\]]'jam pos 'dul ba la yang 'jam por ston/_ma rungs pa rnams la yang khro bor ston te thabs gzhan gyis mthu ma rungs pa rnams la thabs gzhan med na mthar drag shul gyis gdul ba'i phyir/_tha mar nag po gzhag ste khro bo la sogs ting nge 'dzin rnam par sprul pa zhes gong du 
[\[40a.7\]]smos pa de/_de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad de'i tshe ting nge 'dzin de las bzhengs nas zhes bya ba la sogs pa 'di man chad kyis 'chad de/_ting nge 'dzin de las bzhengs zhes pa ni sngar ting nge 'dzin du zhugs pa las bzhengs nas rgyal ba'i ting nge 'dzin la 'jug 
[\[40b\]]
[\[40b.1\]]pa'o/_/mtha' yas pas rnam par rgyal ba zhes pa ni grangs med pa'i rgol ba rnams kyis mi tshugs pas na grangs med pa las rgyal ba zhes pa'i don to/_/thams cad du thogs pa med pa'i stobs zhes bya ba'i rig sngags kyi rgyal mo de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yul las 'byung ba 
[\[40b.2\]]'di 'byung ngo zhes pa de la thams cad du thogs pa med pa'i stobs zhes pa ni kun rdzob dang don dam par te/_nyon mongs pa dang lha'i bu'i bdud la sogs pas mi tshugs zhes pa'i don to/_/rnam par snang mdzad chen po mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa rnam par sprul pa byin gyis rlob pa 
[\[40b.3\]]las/_yi ge'i 'khor lo rim par phye ba rgyas pa'o//_//da ni phyag rgya'i le'u bshad par bya ste/_phyag rgya rnams bshad pa yang spyir 'dul ba'i 'gro ba zab cing rgya che ba rnams mos pa dang dmigs pa de rnam pa gnyis so/_/de la zab cing rgya che ba la mos pa'i don to/_/'gro 
[\[40b.4\]]ba ni sngags kyi tshig rnams bshad pas sngags tshig de bdag gi sgra'i de kho na nyid la dmigs shing byang chub kyi sems kyi bsgom pa sngon du 'gro ba'i lha'i gzugs su sbyor te lha'i gzugs sbyor ba de yang ting nge 'dzin dag pa dang /_rnam par rtog pa gzhan spangs pa dang /_lha'i gzugs 
[\[40b.5\]]su snang ba rdzogs par sbyor ro/_/dmigs pa can gyis gdul ba'i 'gro ba ni sngags kyi sgra'i de kho na nyid la dmigs pa la sogs pa de dag mi nus te phyag rgya tsam bcings nas lha'i gzugs su sbyor bar byed pas na dmigs pa can gyis gdul ba'i 'gro ba de dag gi phyir bcom 
[\[40b.6\]]ldan 'das kyi rgyu dang 'bras bu'i yon tan rnams phyag rgya'i gzugs su byin gyis brlob po/_/de la ral gri dang rdo rje la sogs pa'i phyag rgya ni yid kyi yon tan no/_/dung gi phyag rgya la sogs pa'i phyag rgya ni gsung gi yon tan no/_/gtsug tor gyi phyag rgya la sogs pa'i phyag rgya ni 
[\[40b.7\]]sku'i yon tan no/_/ji ltar rigs par sbyar bar bya'o/_/phyag rgya'i le'u bshad pa de yang /_de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis thams cad dang ldan pa'i 'khor 'dus pa la gzigs nas/_byang chub sems dpa' gsang ba'i bdag po phyag na rdo rje la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/gsang ba ba'i 
[\[41a\]]
[\[41a.1\]]bdag po de bzhin gshegs pa rnams la de bzhin gshegs pa rgyan du gyur pa/_chos kyi dbyings rtogs pa'i mtshan mar gyur pa'i phyag rgya rim par phye ba rgyas pa zhes bya ba la sogs pa ste/_de la de bzhin gshegs pa'i rgyan du gyur pa zhes pa'i gtsug tor dang /_
[\[41a.2\]]spyan gyi phyag rgya la sogs pa sku'i rgyun du gyur pa'i phyir ro/_/chos kyi dbyings rtogs pa'i mtshan mar gyur pa zhes pa ni de bzhin nyid kyi ye shes te de rtogs shing chud par byed pa'i mtshan mar ral gri dang rdo rje la sogs pa'i phyag rgya'o/_/gang gis brgyan na byang chub sems 
[\[41a.3\]]dpa' rnams zhes pa nas/_de rnams kyang de dag gi ngag nyan par 'gyur zhes pa'i bar gyis ni phyag rgya de rnams bstan pa'i dgos pa bshad pa ste/_byang chub sems dpa' gang dag phyag rgya de rnams dang ldan na/_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi byang chub chen po'i 
[\[41a.4\]]mtshan mas btab pas de dag la lha dang mi dang lha ma yin la sogs pa rnams kyis brdzi bar mi 'gyur zhing lha dang lha ma yin la sogs pa de rnams kyang byang chub sems dpa' de dag gi ngag nyan par 'gyur ro/_/ji ltar 'jig rten na rgyal ba'i phyag rgya 'chang ba'i skyes bu de la 'jig rten 
[\[41a.5\]]pa'i mi rnams kyang brdzi bar mi byed la/_'jig rten pa rnams de'i ngag kyang nyan par 'gyur ba dang 'dra ste/_mdor na phyag rgya 'di rnams bshad pas gdul ba'i 'gro ba rnams la mthong ba'i chos dang /_tshe phyi ma la phan par 'gyur bar bstan to/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das 
[\[41a.6\]]kyis de'i tshe stobs thogs pa med par gnas byin gyis rlob pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs so zhes pa ni 'og nas byung ba'i phyag rgya de dag nyid stobs thogs pa med pa'i gnas zhes bya ste/_bcom ldan 'das ting nge 'dzin de la snyoms par zhugs nas phyag rgya de 
[\[41a.7\]]dag 'chad pa na ting nge 'dzin de'i ming stobs thogs pa med pa'i gnas byin gyis rlob pa zhes bya ba'o/_/de la thogs pa med pa zhes bya ba ni bcom ldan 'das kyi sku mi zad pa la sogs pa'i rdo rje'i rgyan de dag ci la yang thogs pa med pa'o/_/stobs zhes pa ni de ltar thogs pa med pa 
[\[41b\]]
[\[41b.1\]]nyid stobs su gyur pa'o/_/gnas zhes pa ni bcom ldan 'das kyi sku la bya la/_byin gyis rlob pa zhes pa ni bcom ldan 'das kyi sku mi zad pa la sogs pa rdo rje thogs pa med pa'i stobs su gyur pa de nyid phyag rgya'i ngo bo byin gyis rlob pa'o/_/bcom ldan 'das ting nge 'dzin de la 
[\[41b.2\]]snyoms par zhugs ma thag tu rig sngags kyi rgyal mo thams cad du thogs pa med pa'i stobs can/_mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i stobs can/_dam tshig la 'jug par byed pa stobs gsum dang ldan pa byung ngo zhes pa la sogs pa med pa'i stobs can zhes pa ni gong ma bzhin no/_/mi 
[\[41b.3\]]mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i stobs zhes pa ni thogs pa med pa'i stobs de nyid gang dang gang mi mnyam pa'o/_/dam tshig la 'jug par byed pa zhes pa ni/_dam tshig gi don ni sngar bshad zin la/_dam tshig la 'jug pa ni sku mi zad pa la sogs pa'i rang bzhin gyi dkyil 'khor 'og ma'i 'gro 
[\[41b.4\]]ba rnams kyi lta ba'i spyod yul du mi 'gyur bas de'i byin rlabs su gyur pa dam tshig gi phyag rgya dang sngags 'di bcings shing brjod pa na rang bzhin dkyil 'khor de mthong ba dang 'dra bas na 'jug pa zhes bya'o/_/gnyis su na srung ba'i dam tshig rnams las nyams pas dam tshig gi phyag rgya 
[\[41b.5\]]dang sngags 'di byas na dkyil 'khor mthong ba dang 'dra ste/_de byas pa tsam gyis dam tshig las nyams nges so/_/slar dam tshig tu zhugs par 'gyur bas na dam tshig la 'jug par byed pa zhes bya'o/_/gsum du na dkyil 'khor du 'jug pa'i tshe dam tshig gi phyag rgya dang 
[\[41b.6\]]sngags 'di byas te 'jug pas na dam tshig la 'jug pa zhes bya'o/_/gsang ba pa'i bdag po rig sngags kyi rgyal mo 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis yang dag par ston par byed pa zhes pa ni rig pa ni brjod pas sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gnas rang bzhin gyi dkyil 
[\[41b.7\]]'khor mthong ba dang 'dra bas na sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yang dag par ston par byed pa zhes bya'o/_/gnyis su na gzugs brnyan gyi dkyil 'khor bris pa yang rig pa 'di brjod nas ston pa'am rig pa 'di brjod nas sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dkyil 'khor de mthong bar 'gyur 
[\[42a\]]
[\[42a.1\]]bas na sangs rgyas kyi sa dang yang dag par ston par byed pa zhes bya'o/_/chos gsum gyi lam gyi mtshams las mi 'da' bar byed pa zhes pa la chos gsum ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku dang gsung dang rdo rje'i yid mi zad pa'i rgyan nam sku gsum ste de'i lam ni dam 
[\[42a.2\]]tshig srung ba'i bya ba la sogs pa ste lam de las mi 'da' ba'o/_/sa dang pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs par byed pa yin te zhes pa ni dam tshig der zhugs nas bcom ldan 'das la mchod pa la sogs pa sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la sogs rim gyis rdzogs par byed pa dang /_
[\[42a.3\]]de'i mthus sa la rim gyis 'phar bar 'gyur bas na sa dang po pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'o/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi dam tshig ni/_/phyag rgya 'di la rab tu gnas/_/zhes pa ni dam tshig gi don dam tshig srung ba las mi 'da' ba'am/_sku mi zad pa la sogs pa 
[\[42a.4\]]rtog par byed pa'am/_sku de dag yongs su thob par byed pa de dag phyag rgya 'di'i sgo nas mtshon cing rtog par byed pas na/_phyag rgya 'di la rab tu gnas/_/zhes bya'o/_/rgya 'di phyag rgya chen po ste/_/chos dbyings rnam par sbyong ba'o/_/zhes pa ni chos kyi dbyings 
[\[42a.5\]]stong pa nyid phyag rgya 'dis mtshon cing rtog par 'gyur bas chos kyi dbyings sbyong ba zhes bya'o/_/phyag rgya dpal dang ldan pa ni/_/chos kyi 'khor lo zhes su grags/_/'jig rten mgon po skyob rnams kyis/_/'khor lo rab tu 'dis bskor ro/_/chos kyi dbyings su 
[\[42a.6\]]byin brlabs nas/_/chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba'i phyir/_/rdo rje sems dpar byin brlabs te/_/dpal dang ldan pa zhes pa ni/_/rdo rje sems dpa' nyid la dpal rdo rje sems dpa' de lung gi chos dang rtogs pa'i chos rnams kyi 'khor lo bskor ba yin la/_phyag rgya rdo rje sems dpar 
[\[42a.7\]]bsgyur na byang chub sems dpa' 'jig rten gyi mgon po rnams kyang chos kyi 'khor lo bskor bar byed do zhes pa'o/_/sangs rgyas skyob pa thams cad kyi/_/phyag rgya chen po ral gri'i rgya/_/'dis ni lhan cig skyes pa yi/_/'jig tshogs lta ba gcod par byed/_/ces pa ni ral 
[\[42b\]]
[\[42b.1\]]gri'i phyag rgya ni chos dang gang zag la bdag med par rtog pa'i ye shes kyi mtshan mar gyur pa ste/_ye shes 'dis ni lhan cig skyes pa'i bdag tu lta ba rnams gcod do zhes pa'o/_/phyag rgya dpal dang ldan pa 'di/_/chos kyi dung zhes rnam par bsgrags/_/'di ni 'jig rten 'di dag na/_/
[\[42b.2\]]sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' rnams/_/zhi ba mya ngan 'das 'gro ba/_/rdul dang bral ba'i chos 'chad do/_/zhes pa ni dung gi phyag rgya ste sangs rgyas kyi gsung gi mtshan mar gyur pa ste/_sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' rnams sangs rgyas kyi gsung 'dis rdul dang bral 
[\[42b.3\]]ba'i chos rnams mya ngan las 'das pa zhi ba thob par byed pa 'chad do zhes pa'o/_/pad+ma dam pa dpal ldan 'di/_/sangs rgyas skyob pa thams cad kyi/_/mi shigs rdo rje'i gdan yin te/_/der ni 'dren pa 'tshang rgya zhing /_/rgyal ba sras bcas thams cad ni/_/'di las skye dang byang chub 
[\[42b.4\]]'gyur/_/zhes pa ni pad+ma'i phyag rgya de/_sangs rgyas rnams kyi gdan yin te/_/mgon po sangs rgyas rnams gang du byang chub pa na yang gdan 'di la bzhugs te byang chub la/_sangs rgyas kyi sras byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyang pad+mo 'di las skyes zhes pa'o/_/phyag rgya chen po rdo rje'i 
[\[42b.5\]]rgya/_/mi shes grong khyer 'jig pa ste/_/sangs rgyas rnams kyi ye shes 'di/_/nyid kyis byin gyis brlabs te bstan/_/zhes pa ni bcom ldan 'das kyi de ma thag pa'i lam gyi ye shes de rdo rje'i phyag rgyar byin gyis brlabs pa ste/_mi shes pa'i grong khyer 'jig pa ni ye shes des sems las 
[\[42b.6\]]byung ba'i 'khor dang bcas pa'i ma rig pa rnams 'joms zhes pa'o/_/gtsug tor phyag rgya chen po ste/_/gtsug tor zhes ni bsgrags pa yin/_/'di ni bcings pa tsam gyis kyang /_/rgyal ba mchog tu 'gyur ba yin/_/zhes pa ni bcom ldan 'das kyi skyes bu chen po'i mtshan 
[\[42b.7\]]gtsug tor de phyag rgyar byin gyis brlabs pa ste/_'di ni bcings pa tsam gyis kyang /_/rgyal ba mchog tu 'gyur ba yin/_/zhes pa ni phyag rgya la ni mang po bsdus pa las lha'i gzugs su 'gyur la gcig pus lha'i gzugs su gyur pa ni gtsug tor gyi phyag rgya ste 'di gcig pus 
[\[43a\]]
[\[43a.1\]]bcings pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi skur bsgyur du rung zhes pa'o/_/rgyal ba dam pas phyag rgya 'di/_/mdzod spu zhes ni rab tu bsgrags/_/'di ni bcings pa tsam gyis su/_/rgyal ba mchog tu 'gyur ba yin/_/zhes pa bcom ldan 'das kyi skyes bu chen po'i mtshan smin mtshams 
[\[43a.2\]]nas mdzod spu zhes bya ba spu'i nyag ma gcig gis brkyang na ni khams gsum thams cad du yang khyab par 'gyur la bskyil na de nyid du zad pa de phyag rgya 'dir byin gyis brlabs pa ste/_'di 'ba' zhig bcings pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi skur bsgyur du rung zhes pa'o/_/lhung bzed kyi phyag rgya 
[\[43a.3\]]ni tshigs su bcad pa nyid kyis gsal bar gsungs so/_/sems can kun la mi 'jigs pa/_/de yis rab tu sbyin par 'gyur/_/gang gi rgya chen de bcings na/_/mi 'jigs phyag rgya zhes bsgrags so/_/zhes pa ni mi 'jigs pa sbyin pa'i phyag rgya ste/_bdag gam sems can 
[\[43a.4\]]rnams bgegs la sogs pas skrag cing 'jigs par gyur na phyag rgya bcings te bstan pas de las thar bar 'gyur zhes pa'o/_/'di ni dam pa byin rgya zhes/_/'jig rten mgon po rnams kyi sgra/_/'di ni bcings pa tsam gyis su/_/rgyal mchog dam pa stsol bar byed/_/ces pa ni bcom 
[\[43a.5\]]ldan 'das kyis sems can rnams la mchog sbyin pa'i phyag rgya ste nam grub pa la sogs pa'i mchog 'dod na mchog sbyin pa'i phyag rgya 'di bcings nas mnod do/_/phyag rgya chen po rgya chen 'dis/_/sangs rgyas skyob pa thams cad kyis/_/bdud ni rnam par bsdigs zhes bya/_/phung 
[\[43a.6\]]byed thams cad 'jigs par byed/_/'di ni bcings pa tsam gyis su/_/bdud sde shin tu mi bzad dang /_/gegs dang log 'dren gzhan dag kyang /_/the tshom med par rab tu 'byer/_/zhes pa ni gegs rnam par bskrad pa'i phyag rgya ste bcings pa tsam gyis gegs la sogs pa thams 
[\[43a.7\]]cad rnam par skrag nas so sor 'byer bar 'gyur te/_de ltar gegs rnams so sor byer na gegs med pas ji ltar bsams pa'i don grub par 'gyur bas na re ba thams cad yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'o/_/'di ni sangs rgyas mgon rnams kyi/_/thugs rje las byung spyan yin te/_/
[\[43b\]]
[\[43b.1\]]mig gi steng du mkhas pas bzhag_/de las sangs rgyas spyan du 'gyur/_/zhes pa sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyi snying rje chen po'i dbang gis shes rab kyi spyan sems can thams cad la gzigs pa ni snying rje las byung ba'i spyan zhes bya ste/_spyan de phyag rgya 'dir byin gyis brlabs pas na 
[\[43b.2\]]phyag rgya 'di bcings te mig tu gzhag na bdag sangs rgyas kyi skur bsgyur ba'i tshe sangs rgyas kyi spyan du 'gyur ro/_/'di ni zhags pa mchog ces grags/_/sdang ba thams cad 'jig par byed/_/sngags pas 'di bcings de yi tshe/_/rtag tu sdang ba bcings par 'gyur/_/zhes pa ni 'phags 
[\[43b.3\]]pa'i lam gyi yan lag brgyad kyi rang bzhin gyi ye shes sems can thams cad 'don par byed pa zhags pa'i phyag rgyar byin gyis brlabs te/_zhags pa'i phyag rgya 'di bcings pas bgegs la sogs pa'i gnod pa rnams 'ching ba'i las byed do zhes pa'o/_/sangs rgyas skyob pa 
[\[43b.4\]]thams cad kyi/_/lcags kyu zhes ni 'di bshad de/_/byang chub sems dpa' bdag nyid che/_/sa bcu dag la gnas pa dang /_/sdang bar sems pa gzhan dag kyang /_/sangs rgyas rab tu 'gugs par byed/_/ces pa ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i rgyu dang 'bras bu'i spyod pa rnams 
[\[43b.5\]]las rgyu'i tshe na bsdu ba rnam pa bzhis sems can rnams sdud cing 'dren pa dang /_'bras bu'i tshe sku mi zad pa la sogs pa'i rdo rje'i rgyan gyis sems can rnams sdud cing 'dren pa'i spyod pa de dag nyid lcags kyus phyag rgyar byin gyis brlabs pa ste/_lcags kyus kyang bgegs la sogs 
[\[43b.6\]]pa gnod par byed pa rnams dang byang chub sems dpa' sa bcu la gnas pa yang ste thams cad 'dren par byed de byang chub sems dpa' sa bcu la gnas pa yang 'gugs na gzhan lta smos kyang ci dgos zhes pa'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i lte ba'i phyag rgya la phyag rgya de nyid las zhes pa ni 
[\[43b.7\]]lcags kyu'i phyag rgya la snyegs te lcags kyu'i phyag rgya las srin lag gnyis bsgreng par shes par bya'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rdo rje'i phyag rgya ni mngon par byang chub pa dang sbyar ro/_/lcags kyu'i phyag rgya ni spyod pa dang sbyar ro/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i thugs kyi phyag rgya dang stobs bcu'i phyag 
[\[44a\]]
[\[44a.1\]]rgya ni kun du sangs rgyas pa dang sbyar ro/_/chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid du rtog par byed pa'i phyag rgya ni mya ngan las 'das pa dang sbyar ro/_/de la sngags kyang /_namaHsa man+ta pud+d+hA nAM/_zhes pa ni kun du sangs rgyas pa dang sbyar ro/_/namaHsa man+ta badzrA NAM zhes pa ni byang chub 
[\[44a.2\]]dang sbyor ro/_/namaHsarba ta thA ga te b+h+yaHzhes pa ni de bzhin gshegs pa dang sbyar ro/_/de la sngags dang phyag rgya rnams bstan pa yang spyir bstan pa dang so sor bstan pa de/_de la so sor bstan pa yang rnam pa gnyis te dngos su bstan pa dang /_gzhan du snyeg pa'o/_/de la dngos su bstan 
[\[44a.3\]]pa ni gong du ming smos pa rnams so/_/gzhan du snyeg pa ni de lta bu la sogs pa zhes pa ste/_rgyud gzhan du snyeg pa'o/_/spyir bstan pa ni gsang ba pa'i bdag po 'on kyang ji tsam du yan lag bskyod pa de dag kun phyag rgya rgyas par rig par bya'o/_/ji tsam du lce bskyod pa de dag kun kyang 
[\[44a.4\]]gsang sngags bstan pa rig par bya'o zhes pa ste/_de yang ji lta bur gnas nas she na/_/byang chub kyi sems dang ldan pa'i yid kyis de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa la gnas te dkyil 'khor bri bar bya'o zhes pa ste/_nam sngags pa byang chub kyi sems dang ldan pa'i yid dang /_de 
[\[44a.5\]]bzhin gshegs pa'i sa la gnas par gyur na ji tsam du yan lag la sogs pa bskyod pa de tsam du phyag rgyar 'gyur ji tsam du lce bskyod pa de tsam du sngags spyod par 'gyur ro zhes pa dang sbyar te/_byang chub kyi sems dang ldan pa'i yid ces pa ni stong pa nyid la dmigs pa'i 
[\[44a.6\]]sems dang ldan pa zhes bya'o/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa la gnas zhes pa ni kun rdzob dang don dam pa rnam pa gnyis te/_kun rdzob tu de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa la gnas pa ni lha'i gzugs su sbyar ba'o/_/don dam pa ni stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhin gyi lhar sbyar ba 
[\[44a.7\]]ste/_de ltar yang byang chub kyi sems dang ldan zhing kun rdzob dang don dam pa'i mtshan nyid lha'i gzugs su sbyar ba ma yin par sngags dang phyag rgya bya ba'am/_dkyil 'khor bris na sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis srung bar mi 'gyur ro zhes dngos su 
[\[44b\]]
[\[44b.1\]]gsungs so/_/rnam par snang mdzad chen po mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa rnam par sprul pa byin gyis rlob pa las/_phyag rgya'i rim par phye ba rgyas pa'o//_//da ni sngags kyi le'u bshad pa la sogs pas sngags kyi tshig dang /_yi ge'i 'bru las ting nge 'dzin gyi sgor 
[\[44b.2\]]'gyur ba la sogs pa dang /_yi ge ka la sogs pa sum cu rtsa gnyis rnams a la sogs pa bcu drug dang ldan par 'gyur ba bstan te/_de la yi ge ka la sogs pa sum cu rtsa gnyis po rnams ni yi ge a dang mi smra ste/_de thams cad kyang a dang ldan par shes par 
[\[44b.3\]]bya'o/_/de yang gsang ba pa'i bdag po thams cad du 'jug pa'i chos kyi sgo chos kyi rnam grangs nyon cig ces pa la sogs pa ste/_thams cad du 'jug pa'i sgo zhes pa ni a dang ldan pa'i yi ge rnams sngags la sbyar ba dang /_ting nge 'dzin la sogs pa thams cad du khyab par 
[\[44b.4\]]'jug pa'i sgo'o zhes pa'o/_/de bas na gsang ba'i bdag po de la byang chub sems dpas a zhes bya ba la gnas te/_dgos pa thams cad bsgrub par bya'o zhes gsungs pa yin no/_/dang po a dang sa dang ba ste gsum smos pa ni rigs gsum smos pa'o/_/de dang por yi ge a 
[\[44b.5\]]tha mal par smos nas ka la sogs pa sum cu rtsa gnyis pa ma smos pa ni ka la sogs pa rnam pa thams cad kyang tha mal pa'i rang bzhin du shes par bya ste/_de dag thams cad byang chub kyi rang bzhin du bstan pa'o/_/yi ge a 'dren pa thog mar smos nas ka la sogs pa thams cad 
[\[44b.6\]]kyang ring por smos pa rnams ni de dag thams cad spyod pa'i rang bzhin du shes par bya'o/_/yi ge aM thog mar smos nas kaM zhes pa la sogs pa sum cu rtsa gnyis smos pa thams cad kyang aM dang ldan pas kun du sangs rgyas pa'i rang bzhin du shes par bya'o/_/yi ge a cho ga gnyis dang 
[\[44b.7\]]ldan pa thog mar smos na ka la sogs pa thams cad a tsheg gnyis dang ldan pa ni thams cad kyang mya ngan las 'das pa'i rang bzhin du shes par bya'o/_/ka la sogs pa'i yi ge sum cu rtsa gnyis po de rnams i la sogs pa dang ldan pa na yang ting nge 'dzin de dag nyid kyi sgo ston pa'i 
[\[45a\]]
[\[45a.1\]]rang bzhin du shes par bya'o/_/de la r-i r-I dang l-i l-I gnyis ma smos pa ni gong du yi ge ra dang i la sogs par ldan pa r-i r-I zhes pa'i khongs su 'dus la/_la dang i la sogs par ldan pa l-i l-I zhes pa de'i khongs su 'dus par shes par bya'o/_/gsang ba pa'i bdag po de 
[\[45a.2\]]ltar na 'di ni yi ge'i tshul la mkhas pa'i chos kyi rnam grangs te zhes pa nas gsang ba pa'i bdag po ngas ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing rab 'byams gang dag na de bzhin gshegs pa rnams thams cad du 'dug pa'i chos kyi rnam grangs 'di mi 'chad pa mthong ngo zhes pa'i bar ni bshad pa'i yi ge 
[\[45a.3\]]rnams kyi rjes su bsngags pa bshad pa ste/_rang bzhin phun sum tshogs pa dang /_lus phun sum tshogs pa dang /_byin rlabs phun sum tshogs pa dang /_mthu phun sum tshogs pa bstan pa'o/_/de lha rang bzhin phun sum tshogs pa ni yi ge'i rang bzhin rnams so sor bstan 
[\[45a.4\]]pa ste yi ge'i tshul la mkhas pa'i chos kyi rnam grangs te zhes pa'i tshig gis so/_/lus phun sum tshogs pa ni gsang sngags kyi tshul la mkhas pa'i cho ga zhes pa'i tshig go/_/byin rlabs phun sum tshogs pa ni nam yi ge'i sngags rnams kyi sgrar brjod dam/_ting nge 'dzin bsgom 
[\[45a.5\]]pa'am/_lha'i gzugs su sbyor bas na de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis byin gyis rlob pa dang /_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis byin du brlabs pa zhes pa la sogs pa'i tshig gis so/_/mthu phun sum tshogs pa ni 'das pa dang /_da ltar dang /_ma 
[\[45a.6\]]'ongs pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis kyang bshad ces pa nas/_chos kyi rnam grangs 'di mi chad pa ma mthong ngo zhes pa'i bar gyi tshig gis so/_/byin gyi rlabs phun sum tshogs pa la las rnam pa bzhi ste/_sngags rnams su sbyor ba dang /_lha'i gzugs su sbyor ba 
[\[45a.7\]]dang /_ting nge 'dzin du sbyor ba dang spyod pa'o/_/de la sngags rnams su sbyor ba ni yi ge'i 'bru de rnams las sngags kyi tshig gi tshul du sbyar ba ste/_gsang sngags kyi tshul la mkhas pa'i cho ga zhes pa'i tshig snga ma nyid kyis so/_/lha'i gzugs su sbyor ba ni yi ge rnams 
[\[45b\]]
[\[45b.1\]]gang rung ba las lha'i gzugs su sbyor ba ste/_lha yongs su gzung ba'i cho ga zhes pa'i tshig gis so/_/ting nge 'dzin du sbyor ba ni yi ge 'bru de rnams la stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhin chos kyi skur sbyor ba ste/_yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi tshul la mkhas pa zhes pa'i tshig gis 
[\[45b.2\]]so/_/spyod pa zhi ba dang rgyas pa la sogs pa'i las dang rab 'bring la sogs pa'i dngos grub bsgrub pa'i spyod pa ste/_byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa'i rol mo ste zhes pa'i tshig gis so/_/ka dang tsa dang Ta dang ta dang pa rnams thog ma dang tha ma dang dbus dang ldan zhing 
[\[45b.3\]]ting nge 'dzin gyi tshogs 'jug pas byang chub dang spyod pa dang yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas dang /_mya ngan las 'das pa dang bcas par thob par byed do zhes pa ni yi ge ka la sogs pa sum cu rtsa gnyis po de rnams dang /_dang po'i a A zhes pa gnyis dang /_tha ma'i 
[\[45b.4\]]aM a zhes pa gnyis dang /_dbus kyi i la sogs pa rnams dang ldan par sbyar na byang chub dang spyod pa dang /_kun du sangs rgyas pa dang /_mya ngan las 'das pa'i sgo ston pa'i rang bzhin yin pas de rnams dang ldan par ting nge 'dzin du sbyar na byang chub la sogs pa thob par 'gyur 
[\[45b.5\]]ro zhes pa'o/_/de bzhin du de bas na gsang sngags kyi cho ga dang po la sogs pa'i tha snyad dang ldan par gyur pa de dag nyid thog ma dang tha ma dang /_dbus dang lhan cig tu gyur par shes par bya ste zhes pa dang po la sogs pa'i zhes pa ni thog ma'i a A dang /_tha ma'i aM a dngaH
[\[45b.6\]]dbus kyi i la sogs pa dang ldan par ka la sogs pa rnams brjod na byang chub la sogs pa 'thob pa 'ba' zhig ma yin gyi sngags kyi tshig rnams su yang 'gyur ro zhes pa'o/_/gsang sngags kyi tshig re re la ji ltar 'dod par sbyar bar bya'o/_/blo yis rnam par rtog na ni/_/de dag

[\[46a.5\]]rnams dkyil 'khor byin gyis brlabs pa la/_sku mi zad pa byin gyis brlabs pa'i dkyil 'khor snying rje chen po'i snying po 'byung ba dang gsung mi zad pa'i byin gyi rlabs yi ge 'khor lo bskor ba'i dkyil 'khor dag ni gong du bshad zin to/_/da ni thugs mi zad pa'i sku'i dkyil 
[\[46a.6\]]'khor chos kyi sku byin gyi rlabs su 'gyur ba de 'chang la de'i zhar la sngar dkyil 'khor gnyis pa bshad pa'i cho ga'i dkyil 'khor gyi mtshan nyid dang /_slob ma'i mtshan nyid la sogs pa dang /_dkyil 'khor gyi yo byad du bcas pa sngar rgyas par bshad cing ma rdzogs pa rnams dang /_

[\[47a.1\]]bya'o/_/mnyam pa nyid ces pa ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku 'byung ba de dag nyid tshogs dang chos kyi sku dang /_sems can gyi don byed par mnyam pa nyid kyi phyir ro/_/bkod pa zhes bya ba ni mang po'i tshig go/_/snying po can zhes bya ba ni 'byung ba'i gnas ting nge 'dzin de

[\[47b\]]
[\[47b.1\]]gyur to zhes pa ni de ltar bcom ldan 'das ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs ma thag tu bcom ldan 'das kyi sku'i ba spu'i bu ga thams cad nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku sems can thams cad dang rjes su mthun pa las dang tshe dang rnam par smin pa'i 'bras bu za ba dang zar 'jug 
[\[47b.2\]]pa la sogs pa smon pa 'byung zhes pa ste/_las kyi rnam par smin pa dang /_tshe'i 'bras bu za bar ston pa ni las legs nyes ci spyad pa'i 'bras bu bde ba dang sdug bsngal rnams la rang rang spyod par snang ba'o/_/zar 'jug pa ni rgyal po dang sems can dmyal ba'i srung ma la 
[\[47b.3\]]sogs pa lta bu'o/_/de dag gi dbang du byas nas gzhan dag bde ba dang sdug bsngal la spyod du 'bab pa'o/_/de dag nyid kyi bye brag gzugs dang kha dog dang zhes bya ba la sogs pas gsungs te/_sems can rnams kyi gzugs dang /_kha dog dang bsam pa la sogs

[\[52a.1\]]bzhin du mos su zin kyang bsgom pa'i shes rab kyis goms par nus pas phyi rol gyi spyod pa rnams la mos par 'gyur te/_des 'jig rten pa'i dkyil 'khor tshon la sogs pas bri bar bcom ldan 'das kyis dbang bskur zhing lung stsal to zhes pa'o/_/'jig rten pa'i 
[\[52a.2\]]dkyil 'khor ni tshon la sogs pas bris nas 'jig rten pa rnams kyi lta ba'i spyod yul du gyur pas 'jig rten pa'i dkyil 'khor zhes bya'i mu stegs pa la sogs pa'i dkyil 'khor la ni mi bya'o/_/de bas na slob dpon dmigs pa can gyis phyi rol gyi tshon la sogs pas dkyil 'khor bri bar 
[\[52a.3\]]dbang bskur gyi/_/sems dbang du ma gyur pas yid kyi dkyil 'khor bri bar ni dbang mi bskur ro/_/zab cing rgya che ba la mos pa'i slob dpon de ni phyi'i dkyil 'khor dang nang gi dkyil 'khor gnyis ka bri bar dbang bskur te/_phyi rol gyi spyod pa rnams la ma chags shing sems dbang du gyur pas 
[\[52a.4\]]nang gi dkyil 'khor bri bar dbang bskur te/_slob ma rnams kyi yid dang sbyar ba'i phyir phyi rol gyi dkyil 'khor yang bri bar gsungs so/_/dus dang dus min bye brag las/_/zhes bya ba nas/_slob ma de ni rgyal ba'i mchog_/ces bya ba'i bar gyis slob ma'i bye brag bshad de/_dus dang dus 
[\[52a.5\]]min bye brag las/_/zhes pa las spyir slob ma rnam pa bzhi las slob ma kha cig sngags kyi rgyud la sogs pa thos shing shes la/_byang chub kyi sems kyang bsgoms pa ni dkyil 'khor du bcug pa nyid kyi tshe na sgrub par dbang bskur la slob dpon dmigs pa can gyis mtshan nyid dang ldan na byang 
[\[52a.6\]]chub sems dang mi ldan yang de nyid kyi tshe na slob dpon du dbang bskur bar bya ste/_de dag ni/_gcig ni de yi mod la bsgrub/_/ces pa'o/_/gzhan ni sngags kyi rgyud la sogs pa thos shing shes kyang byang chub kyi sems ma bsgoms na sgrub par dbang bskur du mi rung ste/_dkyil 'khor der 
[\[52a.7\]]bcug nas byang chub kyi sems bsgom pa'i thabs bstan te/_byang chub kyi sems goms par gyur pa na phyis bsgrub par bya ba ni dus gnyis par bsgrub pa'o/_/de bzhin du slob dpon dbang bskur ba don du gnyer na yang sngags kyi rgyud la sogs pa mi shes na der dbang mi bskur bar sngags 
[\[52b\]]
[\[52b.1\]]kyi rgyud la sogs pa rdzogs par slob tu bcug ste slob dpon gyi mtshan nyid dang ldan pa na slob dpon du dbang bskur ba yang dus gnyis par bsgrub pa'o/_/de la kha cig snga nas kyang rgyud la sogs pa mi shes shing byang chub kyi sems kyang ma bsgoms phyis kyang mi slob la bsgom par mi 'gyur 
[\[52b.2\]]ba ni dkyil 'khor du gzung ba tsam las dus gnyis par yang slob dpon du sgrub par dbang mi bskur bar ni gnyis kar min zhes pa'o/_/mtshan nyid thams cad rdzogs pa yis/_/slob ma de ni rgyal ba'i mchog_/ces pa ni/_rgyud la sogs pa yang rdzogs par shes/_byang chub sems 
[\[52b.3\]]kyang tshul bzhin du bsgom pa zhig gis bsod nams spel ba'i phyir dkyil 'khor du zhugs pa de yang slob ma'i grangs su yang gtogs te/_de ni dbang la sogs pa bskur bas/_/'dir dbang la sogs pa gdod bskur mi dgos te/_de ni/_mtshan nyid thams cad rdzogs pa 
[\[52b.4\]]yis/_/zhes pa'o/_/sa ni dang por shes bya ba/_/zhes pa nas/_de bas rtog pa rnam spangs nas/_/sa kun rnam par sbyang bar bya/_/zhes pa'i bar gyis sa sbyang ba bshad de/_phyi nang gi rnam pa gnyis las/_/phyi'i sa sbyang ba ni sngar bshad zin to/_/'dir nang gi sa sbyang ba bshad de/_/
[\[52b.5\]]sems kyi sar ni bshad pa yin/_/zhes pa ni/_spyad pa sngar ni bstan pa ste/_/zhes pa ni sngar byang chub kyi sems ni rang gi sems yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du shes pa zhes nang /_rnam par ram/_kha dog tu'am/_dbyibs su'am zhes pa nas rnam pa thams cad kyi bar du yongs su btsal 
[\[52b.6\]]kyang mi dmigs pa yin te zhes pa la sogs pa'i bar du bshad pa de'i tshul du ting nge 'dzin dag pa dang /_rnam pa thams cad spangs pa dang /_/rnam par rtog pa dang bral bas na/_/sems kyi dri ma dag pa ste/_/de ltar dag par byas nas phyi rol gyi dkyil 'khor ram/_nang gi dkyil 
[\[52b.7\]]'khor gang yang rung ba bri bar bya'o zhes pa'o/_/de yis de ni yongs su spangs/_/zhes pa ni sngar byang chub kyi sems sbyang bar bshad par des sems kyi sbyang bar bya'o zhes pa'o/_/nam zhig nyes pa dang bral na/_/sems kyi sa ni mi 'jigs pa/_/zhes pa ni ji ltar phyi rol gyi sa'i 
[\[53a\]]
[\[53a.1\]]nyes pa rus pa dang rdo ba la sogs pa med pa bzhin du 'dir yang rnam par rtog pa la sogs pa dang /_'dod chags la sogs pa'i nyon mongs pa rnams dang bral na bgegs la sogs pas mi 'jigs zhes bya'o/_/de bas na/_de tshe de ni dag pa ste/_/nyes 
[\[53a.2\]]pa thams cad rnam par spangs/_/mngon par byang chub mthong bas na/_/de ltar de ni bstan zhes bya/_/zhes gsungs pa yin no/_/de las gzhan du sa spyod pa/_/zhes pa nas/_byang chub sems kyi sa sbyang bya/_/zhes pa'i bar gyis phyi rol gyis rnams sbyangs pa na yang byang 
[\[53a.3\]]chub kyi sems dang mi ldan par rtog pa la gnas te/_sbyangs na mi 'dag go/_/de las gzhan du zhes pa gang gi nang du sbyang ba las gzhan pa ste phyi'i sa la bya'o/_/sngags pa rtog la gnas nas su/_/zhes pa ni phung po dang khams dang skye mched la sogs pa'i dngos po 
[\[53a.4\]]yod par rtog ste/_sbyang bar bya ba'i sa dang /_/rus pa la sogs pa'i nyes pa yang dngos po yod par rtog cing sa de sbyong na de byang chub kyi sems dang mi ldan pas de dag par 'gyur ba ma yin zhes pa ste/_de bas rtog pa rnam spangs nas/_/sa kun rnam par sbyang bar bya/_/nga yis dkyil 
[\[53a.5\]]'khor rnams kyi ni/_/cho ga rgyas par gang bstan pa/_/de dag thog mar bstan pa ni/_/zhes bya ba gsungs pa yin te/_byang chub kyi sems sngon du bsgom pa yang slob dpon gyi mtshan nyid mang ba las shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i tshul la rtag tu mkhas pa theg pa gsum gyi khyad 
[\[53a.6\]]par shes pa zhes bya ba la sogs pa gsungs pa'i phyir/_shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i tshul la mkhas pa ni stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhin la mkhas par mngon par sngon du byang chub kyi sems bsgom par rig par bya'o/_/theg pa gsum gyi khyad par shes pas kyang stong pa nyid 
[\[53a.7\]]la mkhas par mngon te/_gang zag dang chos la bdag med pa'i khyad par la mkhas pa'i phyir ro/_/dkyil 'khor gyi las 'di rnams thams cad kyang bya ba byas pa'i slob dpon gyi mtshan nyid de lta bu dang ldan pas sa sbyang bar bya bas na rtog pa 'ba' zhig la mi gnas [(]gyi[,]kyi[)]/_
[\[53b\]]
[\[53b.1\]]byang chub kyi sems dang ldan pas 'jig rten pa rnams kyi sems dang sbyar ba'i phyir/_phyi rol gyi sa rnams sbyang ba rgya cher bshad pa'i bcom ldan 'das kyi dgongs pa de/_rmongs pa rnams kyis mi shes te/_/gang gis de lta bu'i rtog pa ma spangs pa de ni sangs rgyas dang thams cad 
[\[53b.2\]]mkhyen pa'i rgyur mi 'gyur ro/_/de bas na/_'jig rten 'di na sangs rgyas smin/_/zhes bya ba la sogs pa gsungs la/_slob ma rnams la nan tan du/_/byang chub sems kyi sbyang ba ste/_/de yang smon pa byang chub kyi sems dang /_'jug pa byang chub kyi sems sbyang ba gnyis 'jug 
[\[53b.3\]]pa byang chub kyi sems sngar bsgoms na de sbyangs pa yin pas phyis dkyil 'khor du 'jug kar smon pa byang chub kyi sems sbyin par bya ste/_gnyis po de dag sa sbyang ba'o/_/de ste sngar 'jug pa'i byang chub kyi sems goms par ma byas na dkyil 'khor du 'jug pa'i tshe smon pa byang chub 
[\[53b.4\]]kyi sems sbyin la/_phyis 'jug pa byang chub kyi sems bsgom du gzhug ste de ni slob ma rnams kyi byang chub kyi sems kyi sbyong ba'o/_/srung ba mi g.yo mgon po'am/_/khams gsum rnam par rgyal bas bya/_/zhes pa ni slob ma rnams kyi srung ba gsungs pa ste/_slob ma 
[\[53b.5\]]rnams thog ma nang du 'ong ba'i tshe srung ba dang /_lhag par gnas pa'i tshe srung ba la sogs pa thams cad gnyis po 'di dag gang yang rung bas srung ngo zhes so/_/slob ma nam zhig dmigs pa yis/_/nam yang g.yos par mi 'gyur ba/_/de tshe dri med nam mkha' bzhin/_/yang 
[\[53b.6\]]dag par ni dag par 'gyur/_/zhes pas ni slob ma dag pa'i mtshan nyid gsungs te/_de yang nam slob ma de rnal 'byor la gnas pa'i tshe ting nge 'dzin dag pa dang /_rnam par rtog pa spangs pa dang /_/rnam pa dang bral ba ste/_dge ba dang mi dge ba gnyis ka la dmigs pas mi 
[\[53b.7\]]g.yos pa dang rnal 'byor las langs nas kyang mchod pa la sogs pa'i dge ba ni spyod kyi 'dod chags la sogs pa'i yul gyi dmigs pas mi g.yo bar gyur na de dag pa'i mtshan nyid do zhes pa'o/_/de tshe dri med nam mkha' bzhin/_/zhes pa ni ji ltar nam mkha' gang gis 
[\[54a\]]
[\[54a.1\]]kyang ma bsdus pas rang bzhin gyis dang ba dang /_glo bur gyi sprin la sogs pas chod na yang nam mkha' de rang bzhin dag pa las nyams par mi 'gyur ba bzhin du/_'di yang ting nge 'dzin la gnas pa'i tshe dge ba dang mi dge ba la sogs pa'i dri ma gnyis med pa 
[\[54a.2\]]dang /_ting nge 'dzin las langs pa'i tshe mchod pa la sogs pa'i spyod pa byed kyang sgyu ma la sogs pa'i rang bzhin du shes bzhin du byed de/_dngos po yod par rtog pa'i dri mas mi gos pa'i phyir ro/_/sa byin brlab pa dang po ni/_/rdzogs sangs rgyas kyis bya bar bshad/_/gnyis 
[\[54a.3\]]pa sems kyi dbang gis te/_/'dir ni gzhan gyis mi bya'o/_/zhes pa ni/_sngar snying rje chen pos sa yongs su gzung ba'i tshe dkyil 'khor gyi sa sbyang ba der sangs rgyas lnga bsam pa la sogs pa gsungs pa de nyid 'dir gleng nas rim pa bzhin du bshad pa ste/_dkyil 'khor gyi sa de 
[\[54a.4\]]dang por sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams der bsams nas byin gyis brlab/_de'i 'og tu sems kyis bdag gi bar gzung /_/de'i 'og tu sngags kyis bsrung ba ste/_/de gsum gyis yongs su gzung gi de las gzhan du ma yin zhes pa'o/_/srad bu rnam bzhi bshad de/_/zhes 
[\[54a.5\]]pa nas/_lnga pa nam mkha' lta bur bshad/_/ces pa'i bar ni sngar tshon sna lnga'i srad bu zhes spyir bshad pa de tshon sna lnga gang yin pa'i ming smos nas gsal bar bshad pa'o/_/bar snang la ni bzung nas su/_/der ni dkyil 'khor brtag par bya/_/zhes pa ni dkyil 'khor snga ma la lte 
[\[54a.6\]]ba'i thad kar srad bu gzung zhes spyir gsungs kyi/_der dkyil 'khor bri bar ma bshad pa de nyid bar snang la srad bu gzung /_bar snang der dkyil 'khor bri'o zhes gsal bar bshad pa yin no/_/srad bu dag ni gnyis pa yis/_/sa la dkyil 'khor dgod par bya/_/zhes pa ni sngar 
[\[54a.7\]]srad bu gnyis pa blangs nas kha shar phyogs su blta zhes pa la sogs pa spyir smos kyi/_dkyil 'khor nyid gang du bri ba zhib tu bshad pa de nyid gsal bar gsungs pa yin te/_bar snang la bris pa'i 'og tu srad bu gnyis pa blangs pa des sa la dkyil 'khor bri'o zhes pa'o/_/sangs 
[\[54b\]]
[\[54b.1\]]rgyas rnams ni thams cad dang /_/de'i sras blo can rnams kyi gdan/_/pad+ma yid las byung ba'i mchog_/bkra shis 'jig rten rnams kyis bsngags/_/zhes pa ni gdan pad+ma dkar pos bya'o zhes pa ste/_yid las byung ba'i mchog_/ces pa ni sngar bzlas pa'i yan lag bzhi bshad pa'i tshe snying 
[\[54b.2\]]gar pad+ma dkar po bsam par bshad la/_'dir pad+ma dkar po zhes bshad pas yid las byung ba'i pad+ma smos pas ni yid kyis bsam pa'i pad+ma dkar po de dang 'dra ba'i gdan bya'o/_/rang rgyal dang ni dgra bcom pa/_/phyogs gcig shes par gang grags pa/_/de dag stan du shes bya ba/_/
[\[54b.3\]]pad+ma las byung 'dab ma'o/_/zhes pa ni nyan thos dang sangs rgyas ye shes phyogs gcig pa dang ldan pa rnams kyi stan pad+ma dkar po'i 'dab ma gcig steng du bya zhes pa'o/_/'jig rten gyi ni lha rnams gang /_/zhes pa nas/_rgyal po rnams kyis rab bsngags pa'o/_/zhes pa'i bar du 'jig 
[\[54b.4\]]rten gyi lha tshangs pa la sogs pa rgyal po'i gzugs can de dag 'dod chags dang bcas pa yin pas stan gyi pad+ma dkar por bshad pa yin no/_/phal pa rnams kyi ci rigs par/_/sa yi bye brag dag las bsam/_/zhes pa ni 'jig rten pa'i 'khor phal pa rnams bla ma'i drung na bla ma dang 
[\[54b.5\]]'dra bar pad+ma'i stan mi rigs pas de las dma' ba'i stan rnams gang rigs bya'o zhes pa ste/_nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas dang 'jig rten pa'i lha rnams kyi stan 'dir bshad pa yang 'di rnams dkyil 'khor 'dir ma smos pas zhar la bshad par shes par bya'o/_/dkyil 'khor 
[\[54b.6\]]bar ma la gshin rje la sogs pa 'jig rten gyi lha rnams smos pa yang de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku las sprul pa sha stag yin pas pad+ma'i gdan nyid du rigs par bya'i 'jig rten pa phal pa rnams kyi ni ma yin no/_/mchod pa rnam bzhir shes bya ste/_/zhes pa nas/_de las gzhan 
[\[54b.7\]]pa 'jig rten pa'i lha rnams la ni [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin zhes pa'i bar gyis mchod pa rnams bshad pa ste/_de la spyir mchod pa rnam pa bzhi ste/_/thal mo sbyar ba dang /_byams pa dang /_snying rje dang me tog dang bdug pa la sogs pa dang yid kyi mchod pa'o/_/de la sngags pa zab cing 
[\[55a\]]
[\[55a.1\]]rgya che ba la mos pa'i mchod pa 'di bzhi char spyod de yid kyi mchod pa yang byed/_phyi rol gyi mchod pa yang byed do/_/dmigs pa can gyi sngags pas ni phyi rol gyi mchod pa rnams byed kyi/_ting nge 'dzin ni mi khug pas yid kyi mchod pa med do/_/byams pa dang snying 
[\[55a.2\]]rje ni nan tan gyi mchod pa ste/_phyi dang nang gi mchod pa gang yang rung ba byas nas sems can rnams la byams pa dang snying rje bskyed nas mchod pa de dag nyid sems can rnams la bsngo bar byed pa'o/_/de la phyi rol gyi mchod pa rnams ni sngar bshad zin to/_/da ni 'dir yid kyi mchod pa 
[\[55a.3\]]bshad par bya ste/_de la/_me tog bdug spos 'jig rten pa'i/_/sor mo las byung me tog rnams/_/zhes pa la sogs pa ste/_sor mo las byung me tog rnams/_/zhes pa ni thal mo sbyar ba las me tog 'byung ba de skyob pa kun la dbul bar bya zhes pa'o/_/de nyid dgrol ba'i phyir/_/yan 
[\[55a.4\]]lag kun 'byung phyag rgya ni/_/bcings nas byang chub sems bsgoms te/_/zhes bya ba gsungs te/_yid kyi mchod pa bya ba yang thog mar bdag gis phyag rgya 'di bcings pas ji ltar bsams bzhin du sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' rnams la mchod pa'i sprin 'byung bar shog shig 
[\[55a.5\]]ces smon lam btab nas stong pa nyid kyi ting nge 'dzin la gnas te byang chub kyi sems de mngon du bya'o/_/de ltar ting nge 'dzin la gnas pa nyid na yang yid la nam mkha' mdzod kyi rig pa bzlas te/_de nas phyag rgya de las mchod pa rgya chen po 'og nas smos pa rnams char bzhin du 'bab par bsam 
[\[55a.6\]]mo/_/de ltar bsams na chos kyi dbyings kyi byin rlabs ting nge 'dzin gyi dbang dang sngags kyi mthus de'i tshe sangs rgyas kyi spyan sngar char bzhin du 'bab par 'gyur te/_de bas/_'di ni skyon med me tog ste/_/zhes pa la sogs pa rjes su bsngags pa dang bcas pa gsungs so/_/lha rnams 
[\[55a.7\]]phal ba gzhan la ni/_/zhes pa nas/_ji ltar rigs par rnam par bsam/_/zhes pa'i bar gyis ni gzugs brnyan gyi dkyil 'khor tshon gyis bris pa'i lha rnams la phyi'i mchod pa me tog la sogs pa dbul ba'i rim pa bstan pa ste/_so so'i sngags kyis btab nas thog 
[\[55b\]]
[\[55b.1\]]mar bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad la sogs pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs la mchod de/_de nas pad+ma'i rigs la sogs pa la mchod pa bya ste/_de bas na/_ji ltar rigs par rnam par bsam/_/zhes gsungs pa yin no/_/mthe bo srin lag sbyar ba ni/_/zhes pa nas/_de 
[\[55b.2\]]las gzhan pa 'jig rten pa'i/_/lha rnams la ni [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin/_/zhes pa'i bar gyis mchod pa rnams 'bul ba'i thabs bstan pa ste/_mthe bo srin lag sbyar ba ni/_/zhes pa la sogs pas me tog dbul ba gsungs pa/_'jig rten pa yi lha rnams la/_/lte ba'i thad ka nas shes bya/_/
[\[55b.3\]]zhes pa 'jig rten pa'i 'dir gzugs brnyan gyi dkyil 'khor gyi lha rnams la bya ste/_de'i lha rnams la lte ba'i thad ka nas me tog dbul bar bya la/_'jig rten las 'das pa'i yid kyi dkyil 'khor rnams la ni snying ga nas dbul bar bshad pa'o/_/bdug spos rdo rje'i khu tshur 
[\[55b.4\]]ram/_/yang na pad+ma'i phreng ba yis/_/bar snang la ni bzung nas su/_/'jig rten mgon skyob rnams la dbul/_/zhes pa ni bdug spos gsol ba na rdo rje lta bu'i khu tshur ram/_pad+ma'i phyag rgya bcings pa ste/_/pog phor ni rdo rje khu tshur gyis bzung la kham [(]por [,]phor [)]ni pad+ma'i phyag rgyas bzung ste 
[\[55b.5\]]yid kyis dkyil 'khor gyi lha rnams la bar snang la bstod de bzung nas gsol zhes pa'o/_/gzugs brnyan gyi dkyil 'khor gyi lha rnams la ni lte ba'i thad ka nas rim pa bzhin du gsol lo zhes pa ste/_de bas na/_de las gzhan pa 'jig rten pa'i lha rnams la ni [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin zhes 
[\[55b.6\]]gsungs pa yin no/_/phyi dang nang gi bye brag gis/_/sbyin sreg rnam pa gnyis shes bya/_/zhes pa nas/_sngags kyi shes pa spangs pa ste/_/zhes pa'i bar gyis phyi nang gi sbyin sreg gnyis bshad pa ste/_de la phyi'i sbyin sreg ni bsreg par byas pa ste/_sreg pa'i me la sogs pa phyi'i 
[\[55b.7\]]dngos po gzugs la sogs pa la ltos pa'o/_/nang gi sbyin sreg ni de rnams sems gnyis kyis byas te phyi rol gyi gzugs la sogs pa la ltos pa ma yin te/_de bas na/_las dang tshe las rnam par grol/_/zhes gsungs pa yin te/_phung po dang khams dang skye mched la sogs 
[\[56a\]]
[\[56a.1\]]pa'i chos rnams 'jig par byed pa'i byang chub kyi sems kyis stong par bshig ste las dang tshe la sogs pa dmigs su med par spros pa med pa'i shes rab kyis rtogs te yid la phung po la sogs pa dang sems dang sems las byung ba'i rgyu dang 'bras bu dag dmigs 
[\[56a.2\]]su med par gyur pa de la/_las dang tshe las rnam par grol/_/zhes pa'o/_/de la sreg par byed pa ni spros pa med pa'i shes rab yin la las dang tshe la sogs pa ni bsreg par bya ba yin zhing sreg pa'i me ni rnam pa dang bral ba'i yid la bya'o/_/slar yang sa bon 'byung ba ste/_/zhes 
[\[56a.3\]]pa ni las dang tshe sreg pa'i mtshan nyid stong pa nyid kyi ting nge 'dzin de las langs nas snying rje'i dbang gis lha'i gzugs su bya ba la bya ste/_bdag gi lha gang yin pa'i sngags kyi sa bon las lhar bsgyur ba'i rim pa ji skad gsungs pa'i tshul gyis bdag nyid kyi lha'i gzugs su bsgyur ba 
[\[56a.4\]]la bya ste/_de la dang po phung po la sogs pa 'jig pa'i byang chub kyi sems ni 'jug pa'i byang chub kyi sems so/_/ji ltar 'jigs shing rtog pa de yang spangs te spros pa med pa'i shes rab rnam par mi rtog pa las dang tshe sreg par byed pa so so rang gi rig pa'i mtshan nyid ni gnas pa byang 
[\[56a.5\]]chub kyi sems so/_/slar rang gi sa bon las lha'i gzugs su bsgyur ba ni ldang ba'i byang chub kyi sems so/_/las dang 'dod pa'i sbyin sreg bya/_/zhes pa ni zhi ba dang rgyas pa la sogs pa'i las rnams grub par 'dod pas phyi dang nang gi bya'o zhes pa ste/_/mtshan mar bcas 
[\[56a.6\]]pas mtshan bcas kyis/_/dngos grub rgyal ba dam pa bzhed/_/mtshan ma med pa gnas pas ni/_/mtshan ma can yang bsgrub tu rung /_/zhes bya ba dang sbyar te/_nang gi sbyin sreg phyi rol gyi gzugs la sogs pa la mi ltos pa'i mtshan ma med pa des kyang shing la sogs pa mtshan 
[\[56a.7\]]ma dang bcas pa rnams 'grub par 'gyur ro zhes pa'o/_/phyi rol gyi ni gsum ldan zhing /_/zhes pa ni phyi rol gyi sbyin sreg byed pa na gsum dang ldan par bya ba ni zhi ba dang rgyas pa dang mngon spyod kyi las gang yang rung ba'i kha dog dang tshul du ldan par bya ba dang rdzas rnams kyang de'i kha 
[\[56b\]]
[\[56b.1\]]dog dang ldan par bsams pa dang /_bdag nyid kyang de'i kha dog dang mtshungs par ldan pa'i lhar bsgyur ba ste/_de ltar me dang rdzas dang bdag gsum po zhi ba la sogs pa'i las gang yang rung ba'i kha dog dang tshul du ldan par bya ba ni gsum dang ldan pa'o/_/gnas gsum dang zhes pa ni zhi 
[\[56b.2\]]ba dang rgyas pa dang mngon spyod rnams kyi dkyil 'khor dbang chen dang chu dang rlung gi gnas gsum dang ldan pa'o/_/gsum la gnas zhes pa ni zhi ba la sogs pa'i me thab zlum po dang gru bzhi dang gru gsum dag bsreg rdzas la sogs pa'i gnas yin no zhes pa'o/_/las gsum ni zhi ba dang rgyas pa 
[\[56b.3\]]dang mngon spyod rnams yin la/_lam grub pa ni de dag gi 'bras bu gsum grub pa'o/_/phyi dang nang gang yang rung ba'i sbyin sreg gi cho ga de las gzhan du byas na de dag gis 'bras bu thob par mi 'gyur te/_de bas na/_gang dag gzhan du byed pa ni/_/zhes pa la sogs pa ni gnyi 
[\[56b.4\]]ga'i spyir gsungs pa yin no/_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsang sngags dang /_/zhes pa nas/_lha yi tshad kyang [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin/_/de tsam du ni shes par bya/_/zhes pa'i bar gyis ni sngags kyi lha la sogs pa'i kha dog dang dbyibs dang tshad rnams bshad de/_de bzhin gshegs 
[\[56b.5\]]pas gsang sngags dang /_/de bzhin rdzogs sangs rgyas kyis gsungs/_/dkar dang ser du shes par bya/_/zhes pa ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs ni chos thams cad rnam par dag pa'i mtshan nyid zhi ba'i rang bzhin yin pas na kha dog dkar por rig par bya'o zhes pa'o/_/de bzhin 
[\[56b.6\]]rdzogs sangs rgyas kyis gsungs/_/zhes pa ni shAkya thub pa dang 'phags pa 'jam dpal la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' kun du sangs rgyas pa de dag ni sems can gyi don la spyod pas na tshogs rdzogs par mdzad de rgyas pa'i mtshan nyid yin pas kha dog ser por rig par 
[\[56b.7\]]bya'o/_/phyag na rdo rje thams cad pa/_/zhes pa ni phyag na rdo rje yang bya ba grub pa'i ye shes kyi rang bzhin sems can gyi don du spyod pas kha dog thams cad bar rig par bya'o/_/'jig rten dbang phyug gsang sngags ni/_/las kyi bye brag dag las dkar/_/zhes pa ni spyan ras gzigs kyi 
[\[57a\]]
[\[57a.1\]]dbang po ni so sor kun du rtog pa'i ye shes kyi rang bzhin chos thams cad 'khrul par byed pa'i bdag nyid pas kha dog dkar por shes par bya'o/_/kun du gru bzhi lham pa la/_/[{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du zlum po dang /_/gru gsum dang ni zla gam 'dra/_/dbyibs ni 
[\[57a.2\]]legs par bshad pa yin/_/zhes pa ni lha de rnams kyi 'khor dbang chen la sogs pa'i dbyibs bshad pa ste/_rim pa bzhin du shes par bya'o/_/gzugs ni gnyis su shes bya ba/_/bud med dang ni skyes pa nyid/_/ces pa ni lha rnams kyi gzugs bshad pa ste/_de la lha rnams 
[\[57a.3\]]kyang rnam pa gsum ste rnam par smin pa'i gzugs dang /_gnas yongs su gyur pa dang /_sprul pa'o/_/de la rnam par smin pa'i gzugs ni rang gi las dang bsod nams las skyes pa'o/_/gnas yongs su gyur pa ni sgrub pa pos grub pa'i gzugs so/_/sprul pa ni ting nge 'dzin 
[\[57a.4\]]la sogs pa'i dbang gis gang yang rung ba cig yid la bsams par gyur pa ste/_de dag las la la ni skyes par snang /_la la ni bud med du snang ba'o/_/yang na kun du thams cad kyi/_/kha dog dbyibs dang gzugs rnams te/_/zhes pa ni yang na gong du kha dog dkar po la 
[\[57a.5\]]sogs smos pa dang /_dbyibs gru bzhi la sogs pa smos pa dang /_gzugs bud med dang skyes par smos pa de dag nges pa ni ma yin te de dag kun kyang kha dog thams cad dang dbyibs thams cad dang /_gzugs thams cad du shes par bya'o/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_bsam yas 
[\[57a.6\]]ye shes las byung bas/_/de bas de rnams bsam yas te/_/zhes gsungs te de thams cad kyang de bzhin nyid kyi ye shes bsam du med pa las byung ba sha stag yin pas kha dog dang dbyibs la sogs pa chad pa med pas bsam gyis mi khyab pa'o/_/de yang ci'i phyir zhe na/_ye 
[\[57a.7\]]shes mkhyen gyis ye shes ni/_/sems can don phyir tha dad 'gyur/_/zhes pa ste/_de dag gi kha dog dang dbyibs la sogs pa chad pa med pa yang sems can gyi don sna tshogs la mkhas pa'i ye shes des gdul ba'i 'gro ba dang rjes su mthun pa'i gzugs la sogs pa ston pa'i phyir 
[\[57b\]]
[\[57b.1\]]ro/_/yid kyi rgyan ni ji tsam pa/_/stan dang phyag rgya dag dang ni/_/lha yi tshad kyang [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin/_/de tsam du ni shes par bya/_/zhes pa ni lha la sogs pa'i tshad bsams te/_nang du yid la bsam pa'am/_phyi rol du bri ba la sogs pa kun kyang yid la ji tsam du 'dod pa de tsam

[\[57b.4\]]lus su byin gyis brlabs pa dang phyag rgya des slob ma la sogs pa srung ba ste/_de bsrungs na chos kyi dbyings kyi mtshan mas btab pas cis kyang mi tshugs par 'gyur ba ni 'jig rten na rgyal po'i rgya dang ldan pa'i skyes bu la sus kyang gtse bar mi 'gyur ba dang 'dra'o zhes sngar 
[\[57b.5\]]bzhin du bshad do/_/de bas mdor na chos kyi dbyings/_/mtshan ma phyag rgya yin par bshad/_/ces pa ni mdor na phyag rgya rnams chos kyi dbyings kyi mtshan ma yin par shes par bya'o zhes pa'o/_/dbang bskur gsum du shes bya ste/_/zhes pa nas/_rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas

[\[57b.7\]]che ba la mos pa dag phyag rgyas dbang bskur bya bar bshad pa ste/_dkyil 'khor gnyis gang yang rung ba'i mdun du dbang bskur ba'i dkyil 'khor dang bum pa la sogs pa la mi ltos par kun du bzang po la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa'i phyag rgya dang 'og nas 'byung ba'i phyag 
[\[58a\]]
[\[58a.1\]]rgyas dbang bskur ba'o/_/las dang bya ba spangs pa ste/_/zhes pa ni dbang bskur ba'i bum pa dang /_dkyil 'khor dang dung dang 'khor lo la sogs pa'i cho ga rnams dang bral ba zhes pa'o/_/gnyis pa las dang bya ba yis/_/kun nas bslangs par yang dag bshad/_/

[\[58a.4\]]'khor gong ma gnyis su phyag rgyas dbang bskur nas rjes su gnang ba'i tshig smras pa la bya ste/_phyag rgyas dbang bskur ba'i 'og tu slob dpon slob ma'i rim gro dang nan tan la sogs pas mgu nas khyod kyis deng phyin chad chos kyi 'khor lo bskor cig ces pa la sogs pa'i

[\[58a.6\]]shes pa'o/_/'di rnams kyang 'dir tshig tsam bshad kyis don bsdus pa'i don nyid du gtan la phab ste bshad zin to/_/sangs rgyas rnams kyis mdor na ni/_/dam tshig can rnams sngar bshad de/_/zhes pa nas/_dam tshig can zhes gzhan mi bya/_/zhes pa'i bar gyis ni dam

[\[58b.1\]]dam tshig can du 'dod pa'i phyir 'jug pa ni/_dkyil 'khor 'di gsum las gang yang rung bar zhugs te dam tshig can du gyur na dkyil 'khor lhag ma gnyis la mi ltos la/_sgrub pa dang slob dpon du 'dod pa ni dkyil 'khor gsum char la ltos par shes par bya'o/_/

[\[58b.3\]]la phyag rgya dang sngags sbyin par bya la/_sngar byang chub kyi sems ma bsgoms na de la phyag rgya dang sngags sbyin du mi rung ste/_de bas na/_de la phyag rgya mi bstan zhing /_/sngags kyang sbyin par mi bya'o/_/zhes gsungs pa yin no/_/de ltar dkyil 'khor der zhugs nas phyag rgya dang

[\[59a\]]
[\[59a.1\]]cho ga bzhin du des gzhan la yang bstan par gsungs pa las gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor 'ba' zhig tu dbang ma bskur ba de ni dam tshig shes pa bzhin zhes bya'o/_/gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor dbus su ni/_/sngags pa cho gas dbang bskur ba/_/dam tshig shes pa lngar

[\[59b.1\]]su med la/_kun rdzob tu yang chos rnams 'khrul pa'am sgyu ma lta bur rtogs pas na/_dmigs su med pa'i spyod pa thob pa/_/zhes bya'o/_/rgyal ba'i mchog ces pa ni de ltar kun rdzob dang don dam par chos rnams dmigs su med par rtogs pa de ni bdud bzhi dngos su bcom yang bdud bzhi

[\[59b.3\]]bo nyid med par rtogs te de nyid la sems ri rab bzhin du mi g.yo bar 'gyur na de ni bden pa mthong ba zhes bya'o/_/de dag kyang mdor na thog ma ni 'jug pa'i byang chub kyi sems dang ldan pa'o/_/bden pa mthong ba zhes pa ni gnas pa'i byang chub kyi sems bshad pa'o/_/rgyal ba'i 
[\[59b.4\]]mchog ces pa ni ldang ba'i byang chub kyi sems bstan pa ste de dag kyang mos pas spyod pa'i sa gnyis la gnas pa'i yon tan du shes par bya'o/_/'di bden 'di ni yang dag par/_/zhes pa nas/_sangs rgyas nyid dang gang gzhan no/_/zhes ba'i bar gyis grub pa'i rigs rnams bshad pa 
[\[59b.5\]]ste/_'di bden 'di ni yang dag pa/_/de bzhin du ni mi slu ba/_/ji ltar sangs rgyas bden gzigs pa/_/de bzhin sngon ni ngas ma mthong /_/zhes pa ni/_'di ni byang chub sems thob pa/_/zhes pa ni de nyid kyi rjes su bsngags pa'i khyad par te/_gang yang byang chub kyi sems thob pa de ni bden 
[\[59b.6\]]zhing yang dag pa mi slu ba ste ji ltar sngon gyi sangs rgyas dang /_ngas kyang yang dag mi slu ba yin par mthong ngo zhes pa'o/_/gang gis byang chub kyi sems thob pa yang bsgrubs nas/_de srid du byang chub sems dpa' kun du bzang po'am/_'phags pa 'jam dpal lam/_rdo rje 'dzin 
[\[59b.7\]]chen po gang yang rung ba cig 'ongs te mgo la nyug gam legs so zhes zer gyi bar du bzlas brjod bya'o/_/de nas phyag byas te mchod yon phul na byang chub kyi sems mi brjed pa'i ting nge 'dzin thob par 'gyur ro zhes gong du bshad pa de lta bu'i grub pa thob pa ni byang chub kyi sems 
[\[60a\]]
[\[60a.1\]]thob pa zhes bya ste/_de lta bu grub na de nyid dngos grub yin gyis/_gzhan ci zhig dgos te byang chub kyi sems de grub pa gzhan gyi rgyu yin pa'i phyir ro/_/de las gzhan du'ang dngos grub ni/_/rnam pa dag ni lngar 'dod de/_/zhes pa ni byang chub kyi sems grub pa de

[\[60a.3\]]nge 'dzin gyi khyad par grub pa'o/_/de bzhin sa ni gnon pa dang /_/zhes pa ni sa rab tu dga' ba thob pa las grub pa'o/_/'jig rten mngon shes lngar 'dod dang /_/zhes pa ni 'jig rten gyi mngon par shes pa lnga grub pa'o/_/sangs rgyas nyid dang zhes pa ni sangs rgyas kyi gzugs su gyur 
[\[60a.4\]]pa'i rig 'dzin grub pa'o/_/gang gzhan zhes pa ni rig 'dzin phal pa rnams grub pa ste/_de nyid kyi gzungs kyi grub pa'o/_/gong ma gsum ni sems kyi grub par shes par bya'o/_/rgyud las rgyud du gyur pa yi/_/zhes pa nas/_yid las yang dag 'byung ba thob/_/ces pa'i bar gyis grub 
[\[60a.5\]]pa'i dus bshad de/_de las grub pa'i dus kyang tshe 'di la grub pa dang tshe phyi ma la grub pa rnam pa gnyis so/_/rgyud las rgyud du gyur pa yi/_/las ni mi srid ma nyams shing /_/rnam smin rnam par ma smin te/_/dngos grub dag gis dus su 'dod/_/ces pa ni tshe 'di la grub pa ste/_rgyud 
[\[60a.6\]]las rgyud du gyur pa'i las rnams ma zad de tshe snga ma'i rgyud go drug la sogs pa nas las bsags pa tshe 'di'i rgyud la ma nyams shing bdag gi phung po gson na zhes pa'o/_/rnam smin rnam par smin pa de/_/zhes ston la de ltar yang gson la tshe dang bsod nams la sogs pa'i rgyud bsags 
[\[60a.7\]]pa'i 'bras bu yang 'dir smin par gyur cing snga ma'i las rnams ma zad pas bdag tshe ring ba'i rnam smin rnam par smin pas bdag gi bsod nams kyi rgyud mthu brtas par gyur te/_sgrub pa'i tshe de ltar tshe dang bsod nams gnyis bsrungs na de tshe 'di nyid la grub par bshad do/_/mdor na 
[\[60b\]]
[\[60b.1\]]gang tshe dus gcig tu/_/las dang skye ba 'phang gyur pa/_/de tshe'ang sngags pas dngos grub ni/_/yid las yang dag 'byung ba thob/_/ces pa ni dngos grub sgrub pa ni las dang tshes ma btang ste/_dus gcig tu las dang tshe zad par gyur na sgrub pa'i 'bras bu sngags pa des tshe phyi ma la bsgrub 
[\[60b.2\]]mi dgos par rdo rje la sogs pa'i rigs su skye ba dang rig 'dzin la sogs pa'i skye ba thob par 'gyur ro zhes pa'o/_/yid las yang dag 'byung ba yis/_/zhes pa ni rdzas la sogs pas bsgrub mi dgos par tshe 'phos ma thag tu yid nyid kyis grub pa de dag thob par 'gyur ro/_/grub
